## `client-contract-metadata.types.ts`

Projects native agent and graph schemas into serializable client metadata; SDK-specific inspection remains inside this integration before the generic contract is published. This companion preserves the native input and output types without starting runtime work.

Complete metadata needed to generate an agent client contract.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface AgentClientContractMetadata {
  readonly tools: readonly ClientToolMetadata[];

  readonly events: readonly ClientEventMetadata[];
  readonly scopes: readonly ClientScopeMetadata[];
  readonly waiting: readonly ClientWaitingMetadata[];
}
```

## `continuation-validation.ts`

Exposes graph identity and continuation validators to transport adapters without
importing the agent execution barrel. Validation remains owned by its existing
implementations and preserves their public failure and reply contracts.

## createDeepAgentBucketBackendEffect

Builds a DeepAgents backend from a RELKIT bucket client.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runSync(createDeepAgentBucketBackendEffect(bucket));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const createDeepAgentBucketBackendEffect: (
  bucket: DeepAgentBucketClient,
  options?: DeepAgentBucketBackendOptions | undefined,
) => Effect.Effect<
  Readonly<{
    ls: (path: string) => Promise<LsResult>;
    read: (path: string, offset?: number, limit?: number) => Promise<ReadResult>;
    readRaw: (path: string) => Promise<ReadRawResult>;
    write: (path: string, content: string) => Promise<WriteResult>;
    edit: (
      path: string,
      oldText: string,
      newText: string,
      replaceAll?: boolean,
    ) => Promise<EditResult>;
    grep: (
      pattern: string,
      path?: string | null,
      glob?: string | null,
      maxCount?: number | null,
    ) => Promise<GrepResult>;
    glob: (pattern: string, path?: string) => Promise<GlobResult>;
    delete: (path: string) => Promise<DeleteResult>;
    uploadFiles: (files: Array<[string, Uint8Array]>) => Promise<Array<FileUploadResponse>>;
    downloadFiles: (paths: string[]) => Promise<Array<FileDownloadResponse>>;
  }>,
  DeepAgentBucketFailure,
  never
>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/deepagent-bucket-backend.ts#L26)

## DeepAgentBucketClient (interface)

IO operations required by the DeepAgents bucket bridge.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface DeepAgentBucketClient {
  readonly put: (
    key: string,
    bytes: Uint8Array,
    options?: {
      readonly contentType?: string;
      readonly metadata?: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
    },
  ) => Promise<void>;
  readonly get: (key: string) => Promise<Uint8Array | undefined>;
  readonly head: (key: string) => Promise<DeepAgentBucketMetadata | undefined>;
  readonly delete: (key: string) => Promise<void>;
  readonly exists: (key: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  readonly list: (prefix?: string) => Promise<readonly string[]>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/deepagent-bucket-client.types.ts#L17)

## removeBucketPath

Removes a bucket path for existing DeepAgents Promise callers.

**Throws**

A cancellation or timeout failure from the bucket client.

**Example**

```ts
await removeBucketPath(context, "/notes");
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const removeBucketPath: (
  context: DeepAgentBucketContext,
  path: string,
) => Promise<DeleteResult>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/deepagent-bucket-delete.ts#L63)

## removeBucketPathEffect

Removes a virtual file or directory with at most eight bucket requests in flight.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runPromise(Effect.provide(removeBucketPathEffect("/notes"), deepAgentBucketLayer(context)));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const removeBucketPathEffect: (
  path: string,
) => Effect.Effect<
  | { error: string; path?: never; filesUpdate?: never }
  | { path: string; filesUpdate: null; error?: never },
  DeepAgentBucketFailure,
  DeepAgentBucket
>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/deepagent-bucket-delete.ts#L21)

## `deepagent-bucket-files.types.ts`

Collects bucket-backed file entries for native filesystem operations after path normalization, keeping metadata separate from fetched file content. This companion preserves the native input and output types without starting runtime work.

# utils

## grepFiles

Searches text files for existing DeepAgents Promise callers.

**Throws**

A cancellation or timeout failure from the bucket client.

**Example**

```ts
await grepFiles(context, "TODO", "/notes", "*.md", 10);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const grepFiles: (
  context: DeepAgentBucketContext,
  pattern: string,
  path?: string | null,
  globPattern?: string | null,
  maxCount?: number | null,
) => Promise<GrepResult>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/deepagent-bucket-grep.ts#L85)

## grepFilesEffect

Searches text files in key order and stops as soon as the match cap is exceeded.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runPromise(Effect.provide(grepFilesEffect("TODO"), deepAgentBucketLayer(context)));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const grepFilesEffect: (
  pattern: string,
  path?: string | null | undefined,
  globPattern?: string | null | undefined,
  maxCount?: number | null | undefined,
) => Effect.Effect<
  | { matches: Array<GrepMatch>; truncated: boolean }
  | { matches: Array<GrepMatch>; truncated?: never },
  DeepAgentBucketFailure,
  DeepAgentBucket
>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/deepagent-bucket-grep.ts#L28)

## `deepagent-bucket-scope.ts`

Owns bucket backend access for an agent invocation and closes acquired resources only after the scoped operation settles.

  - [createThreadBucketBackend](#createthreadbucketbackend)
  - [createThreadBucketBackendEffect](#createthreadbucketbackendeffect)

# utils

## createThreadBucketBackend

Creates a private thread backend for existing synchronous callers.

**Throws**

The original invalid bucket or thread error.

**Example**

```ts
const backend = createThreadBucketBackend(bucket, "agent", "thread");
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const createThreadBucketBackend: (
  bucket: DeepAgentBucketClient,
  agentId: string,
  threadId: string,
) => Readonly<{
  ls: (path: string) => Promise<LsResult>;
  read: (path: string, offset?: number, limit?: number) => Promise<ReadResult>;
  readRaw: (path: string) => Promise<ReadRawResult>;
  write: (path: string, content: string) => Promise<WriteResult>;
  edit: (
    path: string,
    oldText: string,
    newText: string,
    replaceAll?: boolean,
  ) => Promise<EditResult>;
  grep: (
    pattern: string,
    path?: string | null,
    glob?: string | null,
    maxCount?: number | null,
  ) => Promise<GrepResult>;
  glob: (pattern: string, path?: string) => Promise<GlobResult>;
  delete: (path: string) => Promise<DeleteResult>;
  uploadFiles: (files: Array<[string, Uint8Array]>) => Promise<Array<FileUploadResponse>>;
  downloadFiles: (paths: string[]) => Promise<Array<FileDownloadResponse>>;
}>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/deepagent-bucket-scope.ts#L41)

## createThreadBucketBackendEffect

Creates a private filesystem partition for one exact agent thread.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runSync(createThreadBucketBackendEffect(bucket, "agent", "thread"));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const createThreadBucketBackendEffect: (
  bucket: DeepAgentBucketClient,
  agentId: string,
  threadId: string,
) => Effect.Effect<
  Readonly<{
    ls: (path: string) => Promise<LsResult>;
    read: (path: string, offset?: number, limit?: number) => Promise<ReadResult>;
    readRaw: (path: string) => Promise<ReadRawResult>;
    write: (path: string, content: string) => Promise<WriteResult>;
    edit: (
      path: string,
      oldText: string,
      newText: string,
      replaceAll?: boolean,
    ) => Promise<EditResult>;
    grep: (
      pattern: string,
      path?: string | null,
      glob?: string | null,
      maxCount?: number | null,
    ) => Promise<GrepResult>;
    glob: (pattern: string, path?: string) => Promise<GlobResult>;
    delete: (path: string) => Promise<DeleteResult>;
    uploadFiles: (files: Array<[string, Uint8Array]>) => Promise<Array<FileUploadResponse>>;
    downloadFiles: (paths: string[]) => Promise<Array<FileDownloadResponse>>;
  }>,
  DeepAgentBucketFailure,
  never
>;
```



## deepAgentBucketLayer

Provides one bucket client and path prefix to an Effect operation.

**Example**

```ts
deepAgentBucketLayer({ bucket, prefix: "threads/one" });
```

**Signature**


# utils

## downloadBucketFiles

Downloads files for existing DeepAgents Promise callers.

**Throws**

A cancellation or timeout failure from the bucket client.

**Example**

```ts
await downloadBucketFiles(context, ["/a"]);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const downloadBucketFiles: (
  context: DeepAgentBucketContext,
  paths: string[],
) => Promise<FileDownloadResponse[]>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/deepagent-bucket-transfer.ts#L109)

## downloadBucketFilesEffect

Downloads files in input order with a result for each path.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runPromise(Effect.provide(downloadBucketFilesEffect(["/a"]), deepAgentBucketLayer(context)));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const downloadBucketFilesEffect: (
  paths: Array<string>,
) => Effect.Effect<
  Array<
    | { path: string; content: null; error: "file_not_found" }
    | { path: string; content: Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>; error: null }
    | { path: string; content: null; error: "invalid_path" }
  >,
  DeepAgentBucketFailure,
  DeepAgentBucket
>;
```

## uploadBucketFiles

Uploads files for existing DeepAgents Promise callers.

**Throws**

A cancellation or timeout failure from the bucket client.

**Example**

```ts
await uploadBucketFiles(context, [["/a", bytes]]);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const uploadBucketFiles: (
  context: DeepAgentBucketContext,
  files: Array<[string, Uint8Array]>,
) => Promise<FileUploadResponse[]>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/deepagent-bucket-transfer.ts#L54)

## uploadBucketFilesEffect

Uploads files in input order so repeated paths retain last-write behavior.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runPromise(
  Effect.provide(uploadBucketFilesEffect([["/a", bytes]]), deepAgentBucketLayer(context)),
);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const uploadBucketFilesEffect: (
  files: Array<[string, Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>]>,
) => Effect.Effect<

  DeepAgentBucketFailure,
  DeepAgentBucket
>;
```

```ts
>;
```

# Agents

## defineAgent

Defines a native agent for existing synchronous authoring callers.

**Throws**

The original invalid authoring or identity error.

**Example**

```ts
import { defineAgent } from "@relkit/langchain";
import { z } from "@relkit/app/schema";

const support = defineAgent({
  id: "support",
  input: z.object({ message: z.string() }),
  output: z.object({ answer: z.string() }),
  instructions: "Reply briefly.",
  tools: [],
  limits: { maxSteps: 4, maxToolCalls: 2, timeoutMs: 30_000 },
});
void support;
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const defineAgent: <
  const Id extends string,
  const InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  const OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  const Middleware extends readonly AgentMiddleware[] = readonly [],
  const Tools extends readonly AgentTool[] = ReadonlyArray<AgentTool>,
>(
  options: DefineAgentOptions<Id, InputSchema, OutputSchema, Middleware, Tools>,
) => AgentDescriptor<
  Id,
  InferInput<InputSchema>,
  InferOutput<OutputSchema>,
  InputSchema,
  OutputSchema,
  Middleware,
  Tools
>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/define-agent.ts#L109)

Since v0.4.0

```ts
>
```

# utils

## prepareGraphDescriptor

Validates graph authoring options and returns an assembler for its identity.

**Throws**

Invalid schema, node, edge, or client policy errors.

**Example**

```ts
prepareGraphDescriptor(options)("support.graph");
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const prepareGraphDescriptor: <
  const Id extends string,
  const InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  const OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  const State extends AnyStateSchema,
  const Nodes extends readonly GraphNodeLike[],
>(
  options: DefineGraphOptions<Id, InputSchema, OutputSchema, State, Nodes>,
) => (id: Id) => GraphDescriptor<Id, InputSchema, OutputSchema, State, Nodes>;
```

```ts
declare const defineGraphNode: <

  const OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  const ResumeSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 | undefined = undefined,
  const Ends extends readonly string[] = readonly [],
```

## runGraphNodeHandlerEffect

Runs input validation, the user handler, and output validation in one Effect.

**Example**

```ts
await Effect.runPromise(runGraphNodeHandlerEffect(options, [], input));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const runGraphNodeHandlerEffect: <
  InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  Ends extends readonly string[],
>(
  options: Pick<
    DefineGraphNodeOptions<
      string,
      InputSchema,
      OutputSchema,
      StandardSchemaV1<unknown, unknown> | undefined,
      Ends
    >,
    "input" | "output" | "handler"
  >,
  ends: Ends,
  input: unknown,
  config?: LangGraphRunnableConfig<Record<string, any>> | undefined,
) => Effect.Effect<GraphNodeResult<OutputSchema, Ends>, GraphNodeValidationFailure, never>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/define-graph-node.ts#L36)

```ts
export interface GraphNodeDescriptor<
```

```ts
  const OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  const State extends AnyStateSchema,
  const Nodes extends readonly GraphNodeLike[],
>(
  options: DefineGraphOptions<Id, InputSchema, OutputSchema, State, Nodes>,
) => Effect.Effect<
  GraphDescriptor<Id, InputSchema, OutputSchema, State, Nodes>,
  GraphDefinitionFailure,
  never
>;
```

Exposes typed LangGraph authoring and delegates descriptor validation and construction before any graph execution can begin. This companion preserves the native input and output types without starting runtime work.

# utils

## DefineGraphOptions (interface)

Authoring options for a native LangGraph agent descriptor.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface DefineGraphOptions<
  Id extends string,
  InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  State extends AnyStateSchema,
  Nodes extends readonly GraphNodeLike[],
> extends DescriptorMetadata {
  readonly id?: Id;
  readonly state: State;
  readonly input: InputSchema;
  readonly output: OutputSchema;
  readonly nodes: Nodes & UniqueNodes<Nodes>;
  readonly edges: (
    graph: GraphEdgeBuilder<NoInfer<Extract<GraphNodeId<Nodes>, string>>, GraphState<State>>,
  ) => unknown;
  readonly limits: AgentLimits;
  readonly stateProfile?: string;
  readonly client?: AgentClientPolicy<(...args: any[]) => unknown, GraphStateKey<State>>;
  readonly controls?: readonly AgentControl[];
  readonly checkpointer?: BaseCheckpointSaver | CheckpointerResource;
  readonly store?: BaseStore | MemoryResource;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/define-graph.types.ts#L79)

## GraphDescriptor (interface)

Inspectable agent descriptor backed by a native LangGraph graph.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface GraphDescriptor<
  Id extends string = string,
  InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 = StandardSchemaV1,
  OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 = StandardSchemaV1,
  State extends AnyStateSchema = AnyStateSchema,
  Nodes extends readonly GraphNodeLike[] = readonly GraphNodeLike[],
>
  extends DescriptorBase<"agent", Id>, AgentRef<Id, InputSchema, OutputSchema> {
  readonly execution: "graph";
  readonly input: InputSchema;
  readonly output: OutputSchema;
  readonly nodes: Nodes;
  readonly workflow: GraphWorkflow;
  readonly instructions: "";
  readonly tools: readonly [];
  readonly middleware: readonly [];
  readonly limits: AgentLimits;
  readonly stateProfile?: string;
  readonly client?: AgentClientPolicy<(...args: any[]) => unknown, GraphStateKey<State>>;
  readonly controls?: readonly AgentControl[];
  readonly clientContract: AgentClientContractMetadata;
  readonly [GRAPH_EXECUTION]: GraphExecution<GraphState<State>>;
  readonly asGraphNode: <const NodeId extends string = Id>(
    options?: GraphAsNodeOptions<NodeId>,
  ) => SubgraphNodeDescriptor<NodeId, InputSchema, OutputSchema>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/define-graph.types.ts#L51)

```ts
  readonly store?: BaseStore | MemoryResource;
}
```

```ts
}
```

Acquisition and disposal configuration for an agent persistence resource.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface PersistenceOptions<Value> {
  readonly id: string;

}
```

## PersistenceResource (interface)

Lazily acquired, explicitly released persistence descriptor.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface PersistenceResource<Kind extends "checkpointer" | "memory", Value> {
  readonly [RELKIT_PERSISTENCE]: true;
  readonly kind: "agent-persistence";
  readonly resource: Kind;
  readonly id: string;
  readonly ownership: "owned" | "borrowed";
  readonly acquire: (context: PersistenceContext) => Promise<Value>;
  readonly release: () => Promise<void>;
}
```

## `graph-edges.types.ts`

Defines typed native graph edge and routing descriptors whose endpoints are checked during graph assembly and compilation. This companion preserves the native input and output types without starting runtime work.

---

## Exports Grouped by Category

- [utils](#utils)
  - [GraphConditionalRoute (type alias)](#graphconditionalroute-type-alias)
  - [GraphEdgeBuilder (interface)](#graphedgebuilder-interface)
  - [GraphEdgeOperation (type alias)](#graphedgeoperation-type-alias)
  - [GraphRoute (type alias)](#graphroute-type-alias)

---

# utils

## GraphConditionalRoute (type alias)

Label based graph conditional route callback.

**Signature**

```ts
type GraphConditionalRoute<Id extends string, State, Key extends string> = (
  state: State,
  config: LangGraphRunnableConfig,
) => MaybePromise<Key | Send<Id, Partial<State>> | readonly (Key | Send<Id, Partial<State>>)[]>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/graph-edges.types.ts#L13)

## GraphEdgeBuilder (interface)

Mutable graph edge authoring interface backed by Effect operations.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface GraphEdgeBuilder<Id extends string, State> {
  /** Adds a static or join edge.
   * @param start - One or more source nodes.
   * @param end - Destination node.
   * @returns This builder.
   * @throws TypeError for invalid or duplicate edges.
   * @example edge.addEdge("start", "finish");
   */
  addEdge(
    start: typeof START | NoInfer<Id> | readonly NoInfer<Id>[],
    end: NoInfer<Id> | typeof END,
  ): GraphEdgeBuilder<Id, State>;
  /** Adds a dynamic edge.
   * @param source - Source node.
   * @param route - Route callback.
   * @returns This builder.
   * @throws TypeError for invalid or duplicate routes.
   * @example edge.addConditionalEdges("start", () => "finish");
   */
  addConditionalEdges(
    source: typeof START | NoInfer<Id>,
    route: GraphRoute<NoInfer<Id>, State>,
  ): GraphEdgeBuilder<Id, State>;
  /** Adds a labeled dynamic edge.
   * @param source - Source node.
   * @param route - Route callback.
   * @param destinations - Label to destination mapping.
   * @returns This builder.
   * @throws TypeError for invalid or duplicate routes.
   * @example edge.addConditionalEdges("start", () => "yes", { yes: "finish" });
   */
  addConditionalEdges<const Key extends string>(
    source: typeof START | NoInfer<Id>,
    route: GraphConditionalRoute<NoInfer<Id>, State, Key>,
    destinations: Readonly<Record<Key, NoInfer<Id> | typeof END>>,
  ): GraphEdgeBuilder<Id, State>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/graph-edges.types.ts#L19)

## GraphEdgeOperation (type alias)

Recorded edge operation consumed by the LangGraph compiler.

**Signature**

```ts
type GraphEdgeOperation<Id extends string, State> =
  | {
      readonly kind: "edge";
      readonly start: typeof START | Id | readonly Id[];
      readonly end: Id | typeof END;
    }
  | {
      readonly kind: "conditional";
      readonly source: typeof START | Id;
      readonly route: (state: State, config: LangGraphRunnableConfig) => MaybePromise<unknown>;
      readonly destinations?: Readonly<Record<string, Id | typeof END>>;
    };
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/graph-edges.types.ts#L58)

## GraphRoute (type alias)

Native graph conditional route callback.

**Signature**

```ts
type GraphRoute<Id extends string, State> = (
  state: State,
  config: LangGraphRunnableConfig,
) => MaybePromise<Id | typeof END | Send<Id> | readonly (Id | Send<Id>)[]>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/graph-edges.types.ts#L7)

# utils

## commandUpdate

Converts a native command update for existing synchronous callers.

**Throws**

TypeError for malformed entry lists.

**Example**

```ts
commandUpdate([["count", 1]]);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const commandUpdate: (value: unknown) => unknown;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/graph-node-validation-value.ts#L75)

## commandUpdateEffect

Converts a native command update entry list into an object.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runSync(commandUpdateEffect([["count", 1]]));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const commandUpdateEffect: (
  value: unknown,
) => Effect.Effect<unknown, GraphNodeValidationFailure, never>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/graph-node-validation-value.ts#L63)

## validateDestinations

Checks native command destinations for existing synchronous callers.

**Throws**

TypeError for invalid or undeclared destinations.

**Example**

```ts
validateDestinations(["next"], "next");
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const validateDestinations: (ends: readonly string[], value: unknown) => void;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/graph-node-validation-value.ts#L31)

## validateDestinationsEffect

Checks that native command destinations were declared by the node.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runSync(validateDestinationsEffect(["next"], "next"));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const validateDestinationsEffect: (
  ends: ReadonlyArray<string>,
  value: unknown,
) => Effect.Effect<void, GraphNodeValidationFailure, never>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/graph-node-validation-value.ts#L15)

## validatedUpdate

Validates a node output for existing Promise callers.

**Throws**

The original schema or record error.

**Example**

```ts
await validatedUpdate(schema, output);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const validatedUpdate: <Schema extends StandardSchemaV1>(
  schema: Schema,
  value: unknown,
) => Promise<Extract<InferOutput<Schema>, Record<string, unknown>>>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/graph-node-validation-value.ts#L121)

## validatedUpdateEffect

Validates a graph node output as a record.

**Example**

```ts
await Effect.runPromise(validatedUpdateEffect(schema, output));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const validatedUpdateEffect: <Schema extends StandardSchemaV1>(
  schema: Schema,
  value: unknown,
) => Effect.Effect<
  Extract<InferOutput<Schema>, Record<string, unknown>>,
  GraphNodeValidationFailure,
  never
>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/graph-node-validation-value.ts#L101)

## validatedValue

Validates a node value for existing Promise callers.

**Throws**

The original schema validation error.

**Example**

```ts
await validatedValue(schema, input, "input");
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const validatedValue: <Schema extends StandardSchemaV1>(
  schema: Schema,
  value: unknown,
  phase: "input" | "output",
) => Promise<InferOutput<Schema>>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/graph-node-validation-value.ts#L165)

## validatedValueEffect

Validates a graph node input or output through its Standard Schema.

**Example**

```ts
await Effect.runPromise(validatedValueEffect(schema, input, "input"));
```

**Signature**


# utils

## runCompiledGraph

Runs a compiled graph for existing Promise callers.

**Throws**

The original graph, persistence, output, or cancellation error.

**Example**

```ts
await runCompiledGraph(options, input, signal, id, trace, 1024);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const runCompiledGraph: (
  options: GraphRuntimeOptions,
  input: unknown,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  invocationId: string,
  traceId: string,
  maxOutputBytes: number,
) => Promise<unknown>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/graph-runtime-execution.ts#L88)

## runCompiledGraphEffect

Runs one compiled graph with persistence and a linked invocation scope.

**Example**

```ts
await Effect.runPromise(runCompiledGraphEffect(options, input, signal, id, trace, 1024));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const runCompiledGraphEffect: (
  options: GraphRuntimeOptions,
  input: unknown,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  invocationId: string,
  traceId: string,
  maxOutputBytes: number,
) => Effect.Effect<JsonValue, GraphInvocationFailure, never>;
```

## selectGraphState

Projects graph state for existing synchronous graph definition callers.

AgentRuntimeError when the selection or one of its fields is unavailable.

```ts
  state: AnyStateSchema,

) => AnyStateSchema;
```

## selectGraphStateEffect

Projects a graph state to the fields declared by a client selection schema.

**Throws**

A defect if LangGraph rejects an otherwise valid StateSchema construction.

```ts
const projected = Effect.runSync(selectGraphStateEffect(state, z.object({ answer: z.string() })));
```

```ts

) => Effect.Effect<StateSchema<Record<string, any>>, GraphStateSelectionError, never>;
```

## createSubgraphNode

Creates an embedded graph node for existing synchronous callers.

```ts
declare const createSubgraphNode: <
  const Id extends string,
  InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
>(
  graph: GraphDescriptor<string, InputSchema, OutputSchema>,
  options?: GraphAsNodeOptions<Id>,
) => SubgraphNodeDescriptor<Id, InputSchema, OutputSchema>;
```

```ts
  const Id extends string,

  OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
>(
```

```ts
}
```

# utils

## GraphWorkflow (interface)

Immutable serializable graph topology for runtime and clients.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface GraphWorkflow {
  readonly version: 1;
  readonly start: typeof START;
  readonly end: typeof END;
  readonly nodes: readonly GraphWorkflowNode[];
  readonly edges: readonly GraphWorkflowEdge[];
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/graph-workflow.types.ts#L30)

## GraphWorkflowEdge (type alias)

Serializable static, join, or conditional graph edge.

**Signature**

```ts
type GraphWorkflowEdge =
  | { readonly kind: "edge"; readonly from: string; readonly to: string }
  | { readonly kind: "join"; readonly from: readonly string[]; readonly to: string }
  | {
      readonly kind: "conditional";
      readonly from: string;
      readonly routes: readonly { readonly label: string; readonly to: string }[];
      readonly dynamic: boolean;
    };
```

## GraphWorkflowNode (interface)

Serializable node entry in an agent graph workflow.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface GraphWorkflowNode {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly kind: "node" | "function" | "subgraph";
  readonly input: JsonValue;
  readonly output: JsonValue;
  readonly resume?: JsonValue;
  readonly ends: readonly string[];
  readonly targetFunctionId?: string;
  readonly workflow?: GraphWorkflow;
}
```

```ts
  events: AsyncIterable<ProtocolEvent>,

  toolIds: ReadonlyMap<string, string>,
  relkitNames: ReadonlySet<string>,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  limits: { readonly maxSteps: number; readonly maxToolCalls: number },
  stateSchemas: ReadonlyMap<string, StandardSchemaV1>,
  failure: () => unknown,
  observe: ((event: ProtocolEvent) => void) | undefined,

) => Promise<void>;
```

## collectNativeEventsEffect

Collects native events with linked Effect interruption.

**Example**

```ts
await Effect.runPromise(
  collectNativeEventsEffect(
    events,
    sink,
    ids,
    names,
    signal,
    limits,
    schemas,
    failure,
    observe,
    abort,
  ),
);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const collectNativeEventsEffect: (
  events: AsyncIterable<ProtocolEvent>,
  sink: AgentContentSink | undefined,
  toolIds: ReadonlyMap<string, string>,
  relkitNames: ReadonlySet<string>,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  limits: { readonly maxSteps: number; readonly maxToolCalls: number },
  stateSchemas: ReadonlyMap<string, StandardSchemaV1<unknown, unknown>>,
  failure: () => unknown,
  observe: ((event: ProtocolEvent) => void) | undefined,
  abort: (reason: unknown) => void,
  eventSchemas?: Readonly<Record<string, StandardSchemaV1<unknown, unknown>>> | undefined,
) => Effect.Effect<undefined, AgentInvocationFailure, never>;
```

```ts
}
```

# utils

## ExecutionSignalClockService (interface)

Replaceable deadline timer and current time boundary.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface ExecutionSignalClockService {
  /** Reads the current wall time in milliseconds.
   * @returns Epoch milliseconds.
   * @example clock.now();
   */
  readonly now: () => number;
  /** Schedules a deadline callback.
   * @param callback - Deadline action.
   * @param delayMs - Nonnegative delay.
   * @returns Opaque timer handle.
   * @example clock.setTimeout(onDeadline, 1000);
   */
  readonly setTimeout: (callback: () => void, delayMs: number) => unknown;
  /** Cancels a scheduled callback.
   * @param handle - Opaque timer handle.
   * @returns Nothing.
   * @example clock.clearTimeout(handle);
   */
  readonly clearTimeout: (handle: unknown) => void;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/signal.types.ts#L19)

## ExecutionSignalHandle (interface)

Live execution signal whose owner must call close once finished.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface ExecutionSignalHandle {
  readonly signal: AbortSignal;
  /** Clears the deadline timer and caller signal listener.
   * @returns Nothing; repeated calls are safe.
   * @example handle.close();
   */
  readonly close: () => void;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/signal.types.ts#L9)

## ExecutionSignalOptions (type alias)

Options whose limits and caller signal determine an execution deadline.

```ts

```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/signal.types.ts#L6)

# utils

## TestAgent (interface)

Owned native agent facade preserving schema inference, approvals and trace evidence.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface TestAgent<Agent extends TestAgentDescriptor = TestAgentDescriptor> {
  readonly model: TestAgentModel;
  readonly failures: TestFailureControls;
  readonly approvals: TestAgentApprovals;
  readonly pending: () => readonly PendingApproval[];
  readonly trace: TestAgentTrace;
  readonly script: (turns: readonly TestModelTurn[]) => void;
  readonly invoke: (
    input: InferInput<Agent["input"]>,
    options?: TestAgentInvocationOptions,
  ) => Promise<InferOutput<Agent["output"]>>;
  readonly reset: () => void;
  readonly close: () => Promise<void>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/integrations/packages/langchain/src/agents.types.ts#L127)
